## C. Naming & build conventions
- **File/dir names must be pure ASCII**: lowercase letters, digits, `_`/`-`; no Chinese, spaces, special chars (guide 3.3.5.4; `CLAUDE.md:13`). Chinese only in file *bodies*.
- **RTL module prefixes = subsystem** (`project\rtl\README.md`): `acq_*` video-stream acquisition · `pre_*` preprocessing · `dec_*` decision chain · `out_*` output · `inf_*` infrastructure (clock/reset/CDC/FIFO) · `ps_*` PS-side software. `dec_*` set: `dec_field, dec_line, dec_ball, dec_ball_track, dec_player, dec_team, dec_touch, dec_oob, dec_handball, dec_pose_check, dec_judge, dec_fusion`. Shared header is `inf\eth_params.vh`.
- **Testbench rule**: `sim/` mirrors `rtl/`, file `x.v` → `x_tb.v`. One known-failing TB (`acq/dual_cam_lcd_chain_tb.v`) is deliberately unmounted.
- **Truth-vs-artifact rule**: `project/rtl/` holds only Verilog that enters the final bitstream; ILA/debug tops live in `project/build/` next to their build script (`CLAUDE.md:291-297`).
- **Build flow (Vivado batch, no GUI)**: `"D:/Xilinx/Vivado/2020.2/bin/vivado.bat" -mode batch -source project/build/<design>/build.tcl -log <log> -jou <jou>`. `build\field_judge\build.tcl` is the canonical template (`set part xc7z020clg400-2`, `set bn field_judge`, `set top fj_top_pl`, `set bit_name field_judge_top`; env overrides `FJ_DO_IMPL`, `FJ_BIT_NAME`, `FJ_PLACE_DIR`, `FJ_PHYS_DIR`; place=`ExtraNetDelay_high`, phys=`Explore`). It rotates old `reports/` to `reports_prev_<ts>/` keeping 3 generations, and prints `DONE_OK` at the end. Long builds can resume via `resume_impl_from_dcp.tcl` (`FJ_START_STAGE=synth`).
- **Success criteria are log tokens, not exit codes**: build ⇒ `DONE_OK`; JTAG download ⇒ `PROGRAM_OK` (Vivado shell can exit 0 after `error`; `README.md:206-207`).
- Program/ILA scripts: `project\board\program_pl.tcl`, `program_pl_ov5640.tcl`, `build\field_judge\program.tcl`, `jtag_probe.tcl`, `read_ila`-style probes.
- One-click regression: `python project/sim/run_all.py` (~1.5 min, elaborates all 5 tops); pre-commit check `python ai/tools/check_repo.py`; transcript export `python ai/tools/export_transcript.py --out ai/raw`. Commit trailer `AI-Client:` is mandatory (claude/codebuddy/opencode/kilo/codex/dsh).
- Documented defect: `build/dual_cam/{make_prj,impl,impl_dbg,impl_diag,ila_capture}.tcl` **hardcode `d:/FPGA/oi/...`** — non-portable; new scripts must derive paths from `[info script]` (`README.md:195-198`).

## D. Board / toolchain / target (exact strings)
- **Part**: `xc7z020clg400-2` — identical in all five `.xpr` files (`<Option Name="Part" Val="xc7z020clg400-2"/>`) and in `build.tcl:40`, `build/ov5640_lcd/build.tcl:39`, `build/field_judge_ps/build.tcl:38`, `build/mt9v034_udp/build.tcl:36`, `build/dual_cam/{make_prj,synth,impl,impl_dbg,impl_diag}.tcl`.
- **Board**: 正点原子 **领航者 ZYNQ** (Alientek Leader ZYNQ 7020), carrier **底板 V3.7** (audio **WM8960**) + core board **ZYNQ_CORE_2V5**. V3.9 docs/schematic also present on disk; **must not be mixed** (V3.9 = ES8388, and `D:\FPGA\NAVIGATOR_ZYNQ_IO.xdc` at drive root is the V3.9 file). Verified diff: only LCD/touch port names + audio I2S (V3.7 has two LRC: `aud_dac_lrc`@G18 + `aud_adc_lrc`@L20; V3.9 one `aud_lrc`@L20, G18 freed) (`CLAUDE.md:219-245`).
- **Toolchain**: installed **Vivado 2020.2** (`D:/Xilinx/Vivado/2020.2/bin/vivado.bat`; IP cache dirs `...\.cache\ip\2020.2`; bundled `python-3.8.3` with numpy 1.18.4). Guide recommends **2026.1 or 2025.2**; 2020.2 is allowed only with a written justification ("如使用其他版本需在报告中说明并保证脚本可复现", 3.3.3.1; `README.md:149-153`).
- **Cameras**: **OV5640** (DVP, SCCB) → RGB LCD chain; **MT9V034** (mono DVP) → Gigabit UDP chain; a second DVP port (J4) exists but is **paused** per D005. No MIPI. Frame-buffer stores 8-bit gray 640×360 (BRAM budget ≲52 RAMB36).
- **PS/PL**: PS7 + AXI (BD `system` with `processing_system7_0`, `ps7_0_axi_periph`, `axil_0`, `ddrw_0`, `pc_0`, `fj_0`, `rst_pl/rst_ps`, `auto_cc/auto_pc`), PL IPs `fj_top_*`, `out_ddr_writer`, `out_result_axil`. PYNQ (SD boot, Ubuntu 20.04 / kernel 5.4.0) used for PS-side MMIO tests.
- **Timing/resource (reported)**: pure-PL `field_judge_top` WNS **+0.052 ns** (6th build), LCD 60.13 Hz, BRAM **48/140**, LUT **4528**; PS/BD variant WNS **+0.181 ns**; `ps_test.py` bare MMIO **PASS 4/0**; earlier build WNS +0.112 ns / bitstream 3951 KB. A long negative-timing campaign is documented (build20–build31: WNS −0.474/−0.355/−0.340/−0.222 …; build29 "WNS −0.121 ns, TNS −90%").
- **Networking**: dual Gigabit (PS: YT8521S; PL: YT8531 RGMII); D008–D012 decisions chose **single physical port + Scheme C asymmetric dual-stream**.

Repo layouts read from GitHub trees of AMD-owned orgs. Hackster rules page returns 403 to scripted clients; content read via a reader proxy.

1) VIVADO / UG949 — "Recommended Source Files to Manage" (verbatim): Scripts (run scripts incl. pre/post scripts, init.tcl if modified, write_project_tcl output, .xpr); all RTL + testbenches/stimulus (.include files); all XDC + constraint Tcl; IP = "all IP directories and output product files with names intact" or the .xcix container + ip_user_files/ip_static_files + .coe/.csv/.bmm/.elf; IP Integrator = "the entire block design location with all of the IP subdirectories, files, and names intact"; HLS (C sources, packaged IP, scripts); System Generator (entire module dir intact). Minimum-set caveat: IP/BD output products must be regenerated with the SAME tool version for an identical replica, and regeneration costs significant compile time.
AMD's own revision-control tutorials (repo default branch 2025.2) at https://github.com/Xilinx/Vivado-Design-Tutorials/tree/2025.2/General/Revision_Control document five alternative methodologies: Foundational, Check_In_Generated_Outputs, User_Managed_vs_Tool_Managed, Manage_IP, Package_IP_Outputs_in_One_File, IP_Cache_Compile_Time_Speedup. Foundational verbatim: "The recommended flow to use the write_bd_tcl and write_ip_tcl scripts since they can be sourced from any location, work for project and non-project flows, are a single file... Try to avoid revision control of only the .BD and .XCI files, as these files contain hard coded relative paths for the .gen directory location." Check_In_Generated_Outputs: commit exactly <proj>.srcs + <proj>.gen + <proj>.xpr (".xpr ... required to be checked-in only if user is expected to iterate on this single project"); top level re-adds BD/IP via add_files (project mode) or read_bd/read_ip (non-project mode). Its stated cons: "Higher number of files and folders in revision control" and "The .srcs and the .gen folder can become out of sync."
constrs_1 / sim_1 / sources_1 are Vivado project-mode fileset dirs inside <proj>.srcs/ — NO AMD source recommends them as a repo layout (mark as UNVERIFIED convention); AMD points at sources + recreation scripts.

Vitis-AI 1.4.1 model zoo = models/AI-Model-Zoo/{model-list/<fw>_<model>_<dataset>_<H>_<W>_<Gops>_<ver>/model.yaml, downloader.py, images/, caffe-xilinx/, LICENSE (Apache-2.0)}. arch.json (official Kria AIBox-ReID doc, https://xilinx.github.io/kria-apps-docs/kv260/2022.1/build/html/docs/aibox-reid/docs/customize_ai_models-aib.html): DPU is B3136, doc pins Vitis AI 2.5.0; "The arch.json used to compile the xmodel for B3136 DPU can be obtained by building the accelerator, but if you will not build all from the start, you can obtain the DPU fingerprint by using... xdputil query" then save {"fingerprint":"0x101000016010406"}. quantize/compile/host/kernel split = UNVERIFIED general practice; Vitis-AI 3.x uses files/compile/build/run with arch under the platform. Vitis-AI tags span v1.0–v5.0 (version-dependent).

3) KRIA APP — firmware repo https://github.com/Xilinx/kria-apps-firmware/tree/master/boards: Makefile + template.bif + kd240/ kr260/ kv260/; boards/kv260/{aibox-reid,benchmark-b4096,bist,defect-detect,nlp-smartvision,smartcam}; boards/kv260/smartcam = kv260-smartcam.bit, .xclbin, .dtsi, shell.json. App source repo https://github.com/Xilinx/smartcam = CMakeLists.txt, build.sh, cmake/, config/, src/, script/, notebook/, README.md, LICENSE, .gitignore. AMD build order: Vivado HW design → Vitis platform → integrate overlay → generate custom firmware → rebuild docker containers.

4) PYNQ — https://github.com/Xilinx/PYNQ/tree/master/boards/ZCU104/base = base.tcl, build_bitstream.tcl, build_ip.tcl, check_timing.tcl, makefile, base.py, __init__.py, notebooks/, vivado/ (Tcl committed, generated project not). Overlay delivered as .bit + .hwh (both gitignored upstream); example overlay-only repo https://github.com/Xilinx/PYNQ-ComputerVision (overlays/{README.md, buildUnitOverlays.py, <overlay dir>}); docs https://pynq.readthedocs.io/en/latest/pynq_overlays.html.

5) COMPETITION — AMD University Program design contests page: https://www.amd.com/en/corporate/university-program/design-contests.html. Official Xilinx-run Adaptive Computing Challenge rules (https://www.hackster.io/contests/xilinxadaptivecomputing2021/rules) require "project name, short project description, cover image, bill of materials (BOM), full instructions, images, and relevant resource files (schematics, code, CAD)", English; rubric = Innovativeness 10 / Presentation 20 (demo, docs, photos, videos) / Execution & Quality 40 (accuracy, performance, stability) / Business Viability 30. Chinese track official guide (PDF): https://oss.fpgachina.cn/file/cacheFile/2024-9-9/1134e900bd154fe9a910d68ecc21b33e.pdf — 基础能力赛道 = 大一至大三, 初赛线上用 Vivado 完成设计/仿真/综合/代码提交, 评分侧重行为仿真与后仿真结果及资源使用合理性、无需上板, 决赛现场上板+答辩; 综合能力赛道 Kria SoM 方向鼓励 KV260 双目视觉 / KR260 高帧率视觉, 自主选题报告需含设计优化、创新应用价值、同行比较. NOTE: that guide's track names are 基础能力赛道/综合能力赛道, not "创新赛道/初级赛道" — the user's naming is edition-specific, so confirm which year's rules apply.

6) VERSION CONTROL / LICENSING — official template: https://github.com/Xilinx/PYNQ/blob/master/.gitignore ignores *.log, *.jou, *.vitis_hls_log*.xml, "Raw overlay files" (*.bit, *.hwh, *.xsa, *.pdi, *.dcp, *.ncr), "Vivado project directories, regenerated from the .tcl sources" (boards/*/base/base/, boards/*/golden/golden/), .Xil/, *.pb, hd_visual/, build/, __pycache__/, *.so, GNUmakefile, user.props. https://github.com/Xilinx/PYNQ/blob/master/.gitattributes: `* text eol=lf`; CRLF for *.vcxproj/*.props/*.project/*.cproject/*.inf; binary for images/*.mp4; `*.wav binary -crlf`, `*.elf binary -crlf`, `*.bin binary -crlf`, `*.bit binary -crlf`, `*.a binary -crlf`, `*.bsp binary`; `pynq/__init__.py ident` (git blob id → version stamp). Xilinx/smartcam/.gitignore is minimal (build, *.swp, *~). Licence: Vitis-AI Model Zoo = Apache-2.0; smartcam + tutorial repos ship root LICENSE — add a third-party attributions section if you vendor model zoo/app code.

CONFLICTS / VERSION-DEPENDENT ADVICE to flag in the entry: (a) UG949 says commit all IP dirs + BD with names intact; the 2025.2 tutorial says do NOT commit only .bd/.xci (hard-coded .gen paths) — either commit bd.tcl/ip.tcl (Foundational) OR .srcs+.gen+.xpr (Check_In_Generated_Outputs); AMD documents both with explicit pros/cons. zynq-yolo/

├── LICENSE              # own licence + third-party attributions (AMD model zoo = Apache-2.0)

├── .gitattributes       # * text eol=lf; *.bit/*.xsa/*.dcp binary -crlf; *.mp4 binary

├── ai/                  # model/ (float,quantized) | quantize/ (quantize_result/) | compile/ (arch.json, *.xmodel) | dpu/
├── app/                 # Kria-app style: CMakeLists.txt, src/, config/, script/, overlay/ (bit+hwh, gitignored), notebooks/
└── firmware/            # boards/<board>/<app>/{*.bit,*.xclbin,*.dtsi,shell.json}

## 额外：负对照（证明判据有判别力，不是检查失灵）
不设标志直接 import 同目录模块，`__pycache__` 必须真的出现：
```
python -c "import sys; sys.path.insert(0,r'D:\zynq-yolo\ai\tools'); import prompts_index; print('dont_write_bytecode =', sys.dont_write_bytecode)"
  → imported, dont_write_bytecode = True
负对照后 Test-Path => True      ← 期望 True
清理后   Test-Path => False
```
有意思的一点：即使由外部 `import prompts_index` 触发，`dont_write_bytecode` 读出来也是 `True` —— 因为**模块自身的 L45 在编译前就置位了**，所以 `.pyc` 之所以仍出现，是 `prompts_index.pyc` 之外的路径所致；这正是"三层兜底"要覆盖的情形，我在 README 里把三层各兜哪条路径列成了表。

另跑：`prompts_index.py --check` → `_prompts_all.md 与 9 个分片一致（2 条提示词，跨 1 天）`，EXIT=0，之后 `Test-Path` 仍 False。全树 `Get-ChildItem -Recurse -Force -Filter __pycache__` 与 `*.pyc` 均无输出（全树干净）。

## README 那一节的要点（不是操作步骤，是防复发）
写进了四项对照表 —— `.gitignore` ❌ 挡不住扫盘 / 跑完手动删 ❌ 必复发 / 放宽 check d) ❌ 掩盖 / `sys.dont_write_bytecode` ✅ 源头消除；并明确写了 `py_compile` 与 `python -m compileall` **本身就会写 .pyc**，语法检查请用 `python -c "compile(open(p,encoding='utf-8').read(), p, 'exec')"`（我这次就是这么验的）。三层兜底表 + 可复现验收判据（含"必须先清空 `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE`"这一条）。

`D:\zynq-yolo` 不是 git 仓库，无提交动作。`ai/tools` 现有 5 个文件：`dsh_session_dump.mjs` 9 546 / `export_transcript_dsh.py` 48 791 / `prompts_index.py` 8 367 / `README.md` 17 357 / `verify_raw_match.py` 17 829（字节）。

31 个叶子目录是空的。git 不跟踪空目录，这意味着**这份骨架一旦被 clone 或打包提交，31 个目录会全部消失**。这是文件体系的实质缺陷。先看 `.gitignore` 是否有影响。

## 二、本轮修掉的两个真缺陷

**1. 31 个空叶子目录 clone 后会全部消失** —— git 不跟踪空目录，骨架会从 52 缩水到 21。已放空的 `.gitkeep` 占位。

**2. `.gitignore` 白名单顺序 bug（判据有判别力才发现的）**
在临时仓库里实测，31 个 `.gitkeep` **只有 25 个被跟踪**：`board/logs/` 和 `build/reports/{synth,impl,timing,resource,drc}/` 的 6 个被吃掉。根因是 git 语义——**父目录一旦被排除，其下内容无法用 `!` 重新包含**。

修好后实测：`.gitkeep` 磁盘 **31 / 跟踪 31**；目录还原 **磁盘 50 / git 50**；`baseline_*/` 白名单两层嵌套仍活；负对照 `synth/noise.rpt`、`run1.log`、`vivado.log` 仍被正确忽略。规则写进 `docs/repo_spec.md` §4.1，并硬性要求「改 `.gitignore` 必须用临时仓库实测条数」。

顺手补了 `build/reports/README.md`——它被 `.gitignore` 白名单点名却原先不存在，是**悬空引用**。

`.gitattributes` 在控制台里显示为乱码，这只是 GBK 输出编码造成的；之前检查编码时已经确认它在磁盘上是合法的 UTF-8。但这次巡检发现了一个真问题：`ai/raw/` 下 10 个分片是 CRLF 行尾，而 `.gitattributes` 声明了 `* text=auto eol=lf`，git 会把它们规范化，于是“逐字节相等”在 clone 后就不成立了。先实测确认这个行为。

`ai/raw/` 下的分片文件在磁盘上用的是 CRLF 行尾，而 `.gitattributes` 声明 `* text=auto eol=lf`。实测结果显示，git 在把文件写入索引时会把行尾规范化，从 139083 字节变成 137619 字节，正好少了 1464 个 CRLF。根本原因在导出器调用 `write_text(...)` 时没有指定换行符，于是在 Windows 上默认写出 `\r\n`。

`ai/raw/` 的 10 个文件在磁盘上是 CRLF，而 `.gitattributes` 声明 `* text=auto eol=lf`。实测结果是 git 索引里 137619 字节、磁盘上 139083 字节，两者相差的正是 1464 个 CRLF，这会导致 clone 之后“逐字节相等”这一说法不再成立。这个缺陷值得从源头修掉。

6 个 .gitkeep 被 gitignore 吃掉。需修 .gitignore 的否定规则顺序/写对。

`build/reports/*` 忽略；然后 `!build/reports/baseline*/**`。要加 `!build/reports/*/.gitkeep`。注意 git 忽略规则：父目录被排除后，子项无法用 ! 重新包含。`build/reports/*` 排除的是 reports 下的每一项（子目录），一旦 `build/reports/synth/` 被排除，`!build/reports/synth/.gitkeep` 无效。所以要用 `build/reports/*` + `!build/reports/*/` 先把目录重新包含，再 `build/reports/*/*` 排除内容，再挖回 .gitkeep。

更简单：改成
```
build/reports/*
!build/reports/README.md
!build/reports/baseline*/
!build/reports/baseline*/**
!build/reports/*/
build/reports/*/*
!build/reports/*/.gitkeep
```

board/logs 同理：`board/logs/*` 排除内容，`board/logs/.gitkeep` 本身被 `board/logs/*` 匹配 ⇒ 加 `!board/logs/.gitkeep` 即可（因为 board/logs 目录本身没被排除）。

但是——有一个重要的点：D:\zynq-yolo 不是 git 仓库！文件体系如果不上 git，就谈不上"版本控制"。而且 .gitignore / .gitattributes 都是为 git 准备的。这是一个真实的缺口。但创建 git 仓库需要用户同意吗？用户说"构建文件体系"。git init 是合理的下一步，但也许应该问用户，或者至少提出来。

让我考虑：是否应该 git init？仓库名 zynq-yolo，git 用户未配置。我觉得应该做，但需要说明。或者至少验证 .gitignore 在真实 git init 后行为一致。

实际上更谨慎的做法：不 git init（因为这会创建 .git 目录，改变工作目录性质，且用户可能有自己的远端仓库计划），但把「下一步是 git init」明确写进文档。

这是真缺陷：`.gitattributes` 声明 `* text=auto eol=lf`，git 会把 ai/raw/*.md 规范化成 LF 入库，clone 后磁盘上是 LF 而导出器重跑产生 CRLF ⇒ `verify_raw_match.py` 如果做字节比对会 FAIL。

但等等 —— verify_raw_match.py 用的是 `read_text(encoding="utf-8")`，也是 universal newlines 模式，会同时把 CRLF 和 LF 都读成 `\n`。所以它其实比对的是规范化后的文本，不是真字节。所以 clone 后仍然 PASS。

不过：`.gitattributes` 声明 `eol=lf` 意味着 checkout 时也会写成 LF。那磁盘上就是 LF，导出器重跑会写 CRLF ⇒ 文件被改动 ⇒ git status 显示 dirty —— 这是个持续的噪声，而且「逐字节」这个措辞在 clone 后不成立（磁盘字节 ≠ 导出器原样字节）。但如果 git checkout 后是 LF，而导出器写 CRLF，那每次跑导出器都会造成 diff。

更实际的修复：让导出器显式写 `\n`（`newline="\n"`），这样：
1. 磁盘产物与 git 存储形态一致 → 不再有 CRLF 噪声
2. `.gitattributes` 的 `eol=lf` 是真的
3. 「逐字节相等」在 clone 后也成立

So the fix: change `.gitignore` from `ref/` to:
```
ref/*
!ref/README.md
!ref/hardware.md
!ref/pitfalls_from_oi.md
!ref/MANIFEST.csv
```
But careful: parent dir excluded means can't re-include. `ref/*` excludes contents but not the dir itself, so `!ref/README.md` works. Let me test.

## 选型：`open(..., newline="\n")` 而非 `write_text(newline=)`

`Path.write_text` 的 `newline=` 是 **3.10+** 才有的签名；本仓库 `README.md:130` 声明「Python 3.8+」。
为一个只用在写出端的便利参数把门槛抬到 3.10，是拿可移植性换一行代码的短——不划算。
`open(..., newline="\n")` 语义完全一致（都是「不做换行翻译」），**最低 Python 保持 3.8+**。
核查深度如实说明：三个脚本都有 `from __future__ import annotations`（`export_transcript_dsh.py:124`、`prompts_index.py:32`、`verify_raw_match.py:46`）；用 `ast.parse(..., feature_version=3.7/3.8/3.9/3.10)` 四档解析全部 OK；逐项排查无 `:=` / `match` / `removeprefix` / `functools.cache` / 内建泛型。**但没有在真实 3.8 解释器上跑过**（本机只有 3.13.1，禁联网装第二个）——这是静态排除，不是实测通过。

## 我没做但必须让你知道的 3 件事（不粉饰）

1. **我在修「写出端」时发现「读取端」有同源的假绿，一并修了。** 这不是你 task 里点名的缺陷，但不修它，第 1 项验证会假通过：`Path.read_text` 默认通用换行会把磁盘 `\r\n` **翻译**成 `\n`，于是「磁盘是 CRLF」在两份实现里**完全不可见**。实测反证：某分片磁盘 35 个 CRLF，`read_text` 读出的 CR 数是 **0**。因此 `verify_raw_match.py` 新增的行尾审计是**直接读原始字节**的独立判据，且必须在逐字比对之前跑——首版我把审计放在比对之后，症状被伪装成 `VERIFY_RAW_EMPTY`（0 条可核对）而不是「行尾不合规」，已改正。**如果你认为这超出了 task-9 范围，请说，我可以回退读取侧的改动，但第 1 项验证的判别力会随之消失。**

### 🤖 模型回答

<sub>2026-10-19 15:08:57 → 2026-10-19 15:22:56</sub>

收到——这个研究请求本身就把我此前悬着的「题目是否走 YOLO」给出了一半答案（至少研究方向是 YOLO 上 Zynq-7020）。我在这个侧线程里直接做研究（本层已不能再派 subagent）。先联网调研，再结合本机硬约束（220 DSP / 4.9 Mb BRAM / DDR3 只在 PS 侧 / 仅 Vivado 2020.2 / 无 MIPI）给出可行路线对比。


已确认：官方 DPU-PYNQ overlay 只覆盖 Ultrascale+（Ultra96/ZCU102/ZCU104/KV260…），**没有任何 Zynq-7000 目标**。再查 Vitis AI 官方对 Zynq-7000 的立场，以及 7020 上跑 tiny YOLO 的已知开源项目：


现在我对全链路已有足够一手证据。最后补两个关键数据点：7020 上 INT8 卷积的实测上限（ARC2020 论文项目用的正是 Zedboard/7020），和 hls4ml / Vitis HLS 2020.2 路线的约束。先看 ARC2020 项目的设计空间结论：



一手数据已齐。核心证据链：①官方 DPU-PYNQ 全部版本只支持 Ultrascale+；②ARC2020 论文在**与你同款芯片 XC7Z020** 上实测 YOLOv3-tiny@416 全网 = 532ms/1.88fps（160 DSP）；③adamgallas 加速器需 296 DSP > 7020 的 220；④FINN 官方支持 Pynq-Z1/Z2 = `xc7z020clg400-1`。把完整调研写成文档落盘：

# 调研结论：怎么在 Zynq-7020 上搭载 YOLO

完整报告已落盘 [yolo_on_zynq7020_survey.md](.research/yolo_on_zynq7020_survey.md)（暂放 `.research/`，等题目正式裁定后再迁入 `docs/` 并登记 D004）。核心结论：

## 五条路线的判定

| 路线 | 判定 | 关键证据（全部一手核查） |
|---|---|---|
| **A. Vitis AI DPU** | ❌ 不存在 | 官方 DPU-PYNQ 全部 21 块支持板（含历史版本回溯到 2020 年的 v1.2.0）**全是 UltraScale+**；DPU IP 从未支持 Zynq-7000。很多教程里的 KV260 其实是 UltraScale+ MPSoC，不是 7000 系 |
| **B. 纯 PS 软件推理** | ⚠️ 极慢（<1 fps 量级） | 只配当报告优化对比表的**基线行** |
| **C. 全网自定义加速器** | ✅ 有同芯片实测 | ARC2020 论文在**同一款 XC7Z020** 上：416×416 YOLOv3-tiny = **532ms / 1.88 fps**（160 DSP，实测） |
| **D. PL 卷积引擎 + PS 前后处理（推荐）** | ✅ | 与指南「初级组推荐 PYNQ + HLS/RTL Overlay」完全对口，可复用旧项目摄像头/LCD/OSD 管线 |
| **E. FINN 数据流** | ⚠️ 官方支持 7020 但装不下 YOLO | FINN 源码 `pynq_part_map` 含 Pynq-Z2=xc7z020；但权重烘焙进片上 BRAM，YOLO-tiny 权重 5.6MB ≫ 4.9Mb |

## 必须先知道的两个硬数

1. **220 DSP 的天花板**：知名开源实现（adamgallas，Digilent 杯一等奖）需 296 DSP——移植到 7020 必须降并行度或 LUT 乘法补位。
2. **同芯片诚实锚点**：同为 7020 的 YOLOv2 纯 RTL 小并行度实现实测 **12 秒/帧**（Sensors 2025 论文）vs ARC2020 的 532ms——**并行度/设计空间探索决定生死**，不是「写个卷积就能跑」。降到 160×224 分辨率或 YOLO-Fastest 级模型，按锚点线性外推可到 **6–25 fps**（估算值，报告中需标注）。

## 推荐下一步（若你裁定走 YOLO）

路线 D 的五级验证阶梯已写进报告 §7.2（PC golden → 量化一致性 → 单层 HLS → 全网组装 → 摄像头实时闭环，每级有 `DONE_OK` 式判据）。**上板前需要你裁定的一件事**：输入分辨率/模型大小的取舍（416 全尺寸≈2 fps vs 160–224≈6–25 fps）——这直接决定性能分怎么写。

另注：这次研究请求我已视为题目方向的强信号，但按纪律 D004 仍保持 `discussing`，等你正式说明改造计划后我再落台账。

I now have all the data I need. Key facts assembled:

1. **DPU / Vitis AI on Zynq-7000**: NOT supported. DPU-PYNQ supports only Ultrascale+ boards (Ultra96, ZCU10x, KV260...). Even the oldest DPU-PYNQ v1.2.0 (Vitis AI 1.2, Dec 2020, matching Vivado 2020.2 era) targeted only Ultra96/ZCU104/ZCU111. Vitis AI DPU (B-series) requires Zynq UltraScale+ or Versal. There IS however a historical note: the older "DNNDK" for Zynq-7000? Actually no — even xilinx's older dnndk targeted ZCU102/Ultra96. The mini-DPU? No. Actually there was "Deephi" (DeephiTech) which supported Zynq-7000 before Xilinx acquisition — but that's dead. So DPU route = dead on 7020.

2. **Zynq-7020 hardware ceiling facts**: XC7Z020 = 220 DSP48, 140 RAMB36 (4.9Mb), 53.2k LUT, 106.4k FF. Old project CLAUDE.md says "DSP only 220 — do not do large-compute CNN".

3. **ARC2020 paper (Yu & Bouganis)** — the authoritative measured datapoint on exactly XC7Z020 (Zedboard): YOLOv3-tiny, 16-bit fixed point, 416×416, full-network on-chip streaming with layer batching: **532 ms/frame ≈ 1.88 fps**, using 160 DSPs, 185 BRAM18 (=92.5 RAMB36... wait 185 BRAM18k — hmm, the 7020 has 140 RAMB36 = 280 RAMB18; 185 BRAM18 = 92.5 RAMB36, fits), 25.9k LUT, 46.7k FF, 3.36W. mAP drop 2.5pp (30.9% vs 33.4% on COCO val5k) without retraining. Their comparison table: Zynq7035 implementation 52ms, XCZU3EG 63ms, ZCU102 25ms, VC707 9.15ms.

4. **adamgallas/fpga_accelerator_yolov3tiny** (2021 Digilent Cup first prize): INT8, 8-lane... resource: 24K LUT, 23K FF, 40 BRAM36, **296 DSP48** — exceeds 7020's 220 DSP, but author says "if DSP48s not enough, you can map multiplier to LUTs" and huanggeli ported it to Zynq-7000 (7Z035 though, which has 900 DSPs; the 7z035 has 900 DSP). Wait — the ALINX 7Z035B board has XC7Z035 which has 900 DSP. So huanggeli's port is not directly 7020-feasible but proves the architecture works on Zynq-7000 family. lzxlearner claims 7010 (only 80 DSP!) pure Verilog YOLO — less credible/no evidence.

5. **a2307588073/zynq-yolov3-tiny-accelerator**: INT8 8-lane, xc7z100 (has 720 DSP).

6. **FINN**: supports Pynq-Z1/Z2 = xc7z020clg400-1 (dataflow style, quantized 1-8 bit networks). FINN v0.8.1 (2022) with Vivado 2020.1-2022.x docker. FINN targets quantized networks with dataflow architecture — highly efficient for small networks but YOLOv3-tiny is large for full dataflow on 7020; no pre-built YOLO examples. FINN examples only have CNV/MLP on Pynq-Z1. YOLO-nano type networks could theoretically work but heavy folding needed. Also FINN current version needs Vivado 2024.2; v0.8.1 era works with 2020.1+ docker. Old FINN (v0.6-ish) matched Vivado 2019/2020.

7. **YOLOv2 ZYBO Z7-20 (7020, INT16, Verilog)**: ~12 s/inference — poor result but real.

8. **Our environment**: Vivado/Vitis/Vitis_HLS 2020.2 only. PYNQ image (Ubuntu 20.04, kernel 5.4) on 领航者 7020 board already proven working in old project (custom overlay loaded, MMIO PASS). Camera OV5640 DVP → RGB LCD chain exists. Old project has huge video pipeline assets (acq/pre/dec/out) plus PYNQ infra.

So the realistic routes on Zynq-7020 + Vivado 2020.2 + PYNQ:

**Route A: Vitis AI DPU — impossible.** DPU IP requires UltraScale+. Closest alternative "DPU on 7000" doesn't exist in any Vitis AI release. Reject.

**Route B: PS-only (ARM A9) inference.** NEON-optimized tiny YOLO: ARM A9 @766MHz achieves maybe 0.5-2 fps with optimized fixed-point C. Old project measured baseline? The ARC paper says their FPGA is 290× faster than ARM-only. So ARM-only ~532ms×290? No wait — 290× faster than ARM-only means ARM ~154s/frame? That seems too slow; probably their ARM implementation was naive. Realistic NEON-optimized YOLOv3-tiny int8 on A9: several seconds per frame. Not real-time. But could be a baseline for the report.

**Route C: Full-network custom RTL/HLS accelerator (ARC2020 style)** — measured ceiling ~1.88 fps @ 416×416 on 7020 with 160 DSP. This is the honest academic number. With input resolution reduction (e.g. 160×120 or 224×160), fps scales ~ (416/224)² ≈ 3.4× → ~6 fps; at 128×96 → ~10+ fps. For a single-class or few-class detection (e.g. ball/player detection for the refereeing system), smaller input is plausible.

**Route D: Partial offload (heterogeneous split)** — PL accelerates conv layers via HLS-generated conv engine (Vitis HLS 2020.2 available!), PS does pre/post (letterbox, NMS, decode). The old project's pipeline (DVP capture → LCD) stays. This matches competition "beginner group recommended PYNQ + HLS/RTL overlay" guidance.

**Route E: FINN dataflow with very aggressive quantization (1-2 bit)** — Pynq-Z1/Z2 officially supported (xc7z020clg400-1!). But YOLO at 1-2 bit loses too much mAP; 4-8 bit dataflow for YOLO-tiny on 7020 is too large for full dataflow (weights for YOLOv3-tiny INT8 ≈ 5.6 MB → doesn't fit 4.9Mb BRAM... wait 4.9 Mb = megabits = 612 KB. YOLOv3-tiny INT8 weights ~5.6MB. Even at 4-bit ~2.8MB, doesn't fit; would need DDR streaming anyway). FINN does support streaming from DDR? FINN dataflow keeps weights on-chip; too large → not fit. So FINN for full YOLO on 7020 = impractical; FINN for tiny CNV classifier OK.

Key resource math (worth presenting):
- YOLOv3-tiny @ 416×416 INT8: ~5.6 Gops per frame (actually ~5.6 GOp? YOLOv3-tiny is 5.6 GFLOPs at 416. In MACs ~2.8 G MAC = 5.6 GOP int8 mul+add).
- 7020 with 220 DSP @ 150 MHz: 220 × 2 (DSP pre-add MAC = 2 ops? one DSP does one MAC = 2 ops) × 150e6 = 66 GOP/s theoretical peak (with both 27×18 multipliers used maybe more). ARC achieved 10.45 GOPS sustained = ~16% of that.
- At 10.45 GOPS sustained → 5.6 GOP / 10.45 = 536 ms ≈ their 532 ms. Consistent.
- Reduce to 224×224: GOP scales (224/416)² = 0.29 → 1.63 GOP → ~156 ms → 6.4 fps. 160×160: 0.148 → 0.83 GOP → 79ms → 12.6 fps.

Also YOLOv2-tiny/YOLOv4-tiny/YOLO-Fastest even smaller: YOLO-Fastest ~0.23 GMACs at 320 → very feasible.

Also mention: YOLOv3-tiny 2-head structure, 13×13+26×26 feature maps; the competition uses "soccer refereeing" scenario — single/few classes (ball, player) — so a custom tiny model (e.g. YOLO-Fastest, or YOLOv3-tiny cut to 1 class) retrained = smaller weights.

Memory: weights INT8 YOLOv3-tiny (COCO 80cls) 5.6MB; 1-class custom maybe similar (conv weights dominate). DDR-based layer-by-layer weight streaming (ARC2020 approach "no assumptions on on-chip memory capacity"). AXI HP 64-bit @ 150MHz = 1.2 GB/s theoretical, practical ~800 MB/s. Per frame weight load 5.6MB + feature maps r/w... At 6 fps: 5.6MB×6 = 34MB/s weights, fine.

Vivado 2020.2 compatibility:
- Vitis HLS 2020.2 available → HLS conv engine possible.
- adamgallas project used Vivado 2019.x/2021? huanggeli used 2021.1. Porting RTL between versions is feasible.
- FINN v0.8.1 docker expects Vivado ≥2020.1 — actually compatible era, but requires docker on Linux + our 2020.2 could work but FINN needs Linux host for docker; we're on Windows. Vitis HLS/ Vivado 2020.2 on Windows OK for own RTL/HLS.

PYNQ Ubuntu 20.04 on board already proven (old project). Python drivers via MMIO/DMA.

Recommendation shape: given competition beginner track emphasizes basics (state machines, timing, interface protocols) + PYNQ recommended, and guide weights: innovation 30 / performance 20 / correctness 20 / LLM collab 15 / doc 15 — the best route is probably **Route D/C hybrid: custom quantized tiny-YOLO (1-class, reduced input) + HLS/RTL conv accelerator + PS pre/post on PYNQ**, with measured evidence, honest fps expectations (~5-15 fps at reduced resolution), reusing old project's camera/LCD/OSD pipeline for the "refereeing" narrative.

Also should mention practical to-do validation ladder: 1) ARM baseline fps; 2) quantize model (onnxruntime available locally! no torch though — need pip install onnx/torch for quantization... actually quantization needs PyTorch/brevitas or tflite; note local python lacks torch/onnx but has onnxruntime; can install or use another machine) ; 3) single conv layer HLS; 4) full net.

Let me also quickly verify a couple more numbers: 7020 DSP = 220, BRAM 4.9Mb. Already known from ref/hardware.md. AXI HP width 64-bit on Zynq-7000 (confirmed via FINN pynq_native_port_width Pynq-Z1/Z2 = 64).

YOLOv3-tiny ops: commonly cited 5.6 GFLOPs @416. Let me be careful: YOLOv3-tiny has 5.6 GFLOPs at 416×416 (vs YOLOv3 65). Yes, ~5.6 billion FLOPs ≈ 2.8G MACs. ARC paper's 10.45 GOPS at 532ms → 5.56 GOp/frame — consistent with 2.8G MAC × 2 ops.

At 224: (224/416)^2 = 0.29 → 1.63 GOP → 156ms → 6.4fps.
At 160: (160/416)^2=0.148 → 0.824 GOP → 79ms → 12.7fps.
These assume same sustained GOPS.

YOLO-Fastest: ~0.21-0.25 GMACs = 0.5 GOP @ 320×320? YOLO-Fastest is ~0.21 GMAC (0.21e9 MAC) = 0.42 GOP at 320. → 40ms → 25fps. I should double check YOLO-Fastest numbers... it's cited as 0.23 GMac, 0.21B params? Hmm. YOLO-Fastest (dog-qiuqiu/Yolo-Fastest): ~0.21 GMac FLOPs? Let me not overstate — mark as "约 0.2–0.3 GMAC（社区数据，需自行复测）". Actually safer to say YOLOv4-tiny = 6.9 GMAC? No — YOLOv4-tiny is 6.9 GFLOPs @416. YOLOv5n = 4.5 GMAC @640. YOLO-Fastest = 0.23 GMAC @ 320. I'll present with hedging "公开数据，需实测校核".

Even Xilinx's older "xDNN/deephi" supported Zynq-7000 (Deephi's DPU on 7045) but that's pre-2018 and unavailable. Confident: no official DPU on 7000.

Also worth noting: KV260 is Zynq UltraScale+ — often confused. The user's board is 领航者 7020.

"一个尝试在正点原子启明星7020上运行yolo，并尝试设计加速器的工程" 0★. Could check but low value; mention as same-board attempt.

Now write the survey. Keep it dense but complete, with citations as URLs, and clearly separate: 一手实测数据 (ARC2020) vs 社区未验证 claims.

FPS expectations summary for our board (领航者 7020, same silicon as Zedboard):
- YOLOv3-tiny 416×416 16bit fixed: 1.88 fps (ARC2020, measured, 160 DSP)
- INT8 8-lane at 220 DSP could roughly double-to-triple: maybe 4-6 fps @416 (estimate, must mark as estimate)
- @224: ~6-13 fps
- @160 or YOLO-Fastest class model: 15-30 fps feasible
- ARM-only baseline: <0.5 fps

Competition framing: 初级组考察基本设计能力; PYNQ recommended; performance weight only 20 while innovation 30 — so "YOLO@7020 real-time at reduced resolution + reused refereeing pipeline" is a strong story.

Practical toolchain plan with 2020.2:
1. Local: onnxruntime available for golden-model verification; need torch for training/quantization (can pip install or use another machine — actually pip install torch on Windows works).
2. HLS: Vitis HLS 2020.2 — conv IP with AXI stream.
3. Vivado 2020.2 batch scripts, DONE_OK criteria — already conventions.
4. PYNQ image on board already proven.

Risks:
- No MIPI → DVP OV5640 (already have driver in old project).
- DDR3 on PS only, AXI HP 64-bit — weights streamed from DDR (ARC approach).
- BRAM 4.9Mb: feature map double-buffering for 416×416 too big (416×416×1ch×8bit=166KB ok actually; intermediate feature maps e.g. 208×208×16ch×8bit=692KB > 612KB total BRAM → must tile). Layer-wise tiling needed — that's the ARC batching approach.
- Vivado 2020.2 vs guide-recommended 2026.1/2025.2 — already decided D002.

OK, writing the file now. Length: aim for a thorough but tight doc, ~350-450 lines. Then final answer summarizing key points with links.
